### Task 13: `Session`: debug operations, states, `PROTECTED`, flash lock, `abort()` detaching

The vendor-command parser gets operations 0x09–0x12 and the states DebugHalted (3) and DebugRunning (4). Rules, in the order the parser checks them (the Phase A rule "the state error wins over the size error" carries over):

- Debug operations outside a debug session: `BAD_STATE`. Operations that need a halted CPU while it runs (`DBG_RESUME`, `DBG_STEP`, `DBG_READ_REGS`, `DBG_WRITE_REG`, `DBG_BP_SET`, `DBG_BP_CLEAR`, `READ_MEM`, `WRITE_MEM`): `RUNNING`. Then wrong sizes: `BAD_ARG`.
- `DBG_ATTACH` (12 argument bytes) closes whatever session is open first, as `CONNECT` does. `flags` bit 0 (full mode) belongs to B2 and is `BAD_ARG` here, as is any other flag. The reply is the `CONNECT` identity block, `u32 pc`, `u8 stop_reason`, `u8 hw_breakpoints` (15 bytes).
- In a debug session `WRITE_MEM` may write only the peripheral space below 0x1000 without the flash controller (0x0140–0x014F), and RAM. The RAM window is the **intersection** of the range the host passes and the RAM the probe knows for the device it identified (F5529, ID 0x2955: 0x1C00–0x43FF); for an unknown device it is empty. A wrong host table can therefore only narrow the window, never open flash. Everything else is `PROTECTED`.
- In a debug session `ERASE` and `FLASH_BEGIN` are `PROTECTED`; `FLASH_DATA` and `FLASH_END` stay `BAD_STATE` (no write is open); `DISCONNECT` is `BAD_STATE` (a debug session ends with `DBG_DETACH`).
- `abort()` (USB loss, SWD takeover, a new `CONNECT` or `DBG_ATTACH`) ends a debug session with `DetachAction::Run`: the program continues where it stopped.
- `INFO` reports protocol version 2, feature bit 1 (debugging) and, as a ninth byte, the number of hardware breakpoints. Hosts that read only the first eight bytes are unaffected.

After this task the firmware does not build until Task 14 updates the glue (the `Session` constructor changes); the host tests do not compile the glue.

Create `tests/host/test_msp430_session_debug.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "testing.hpp"
#include "fake_tap.hpp"
#include "msp430/session.hpp"

#include <string>
#include <vector>

using msp430::State;

namespace {

using Bytes = std::vector<uint8_t>;

const Bytes kOk = {0x90, 0x00};
const Bytes kBadArg = {0x90, 0x01};
const Bytes kBadState = {0x90, 0x02};
const Bytes kTargetError = {0x90, 0x06};
const Bytes kProtected = {0x90, 0x09};
const Bytes kRunning = {0x90, 0x0A};

void le32(Bytes &b, uint32_t v)
{
	for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
		b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> (8 * i)));
	}
}

/* DBG_ATTACH: mode, flags, 250 kHz, the F5529's RAM */
Bytes attach_req(uint8_t mode = 0, uint8_t flags = 0, uint32_t ram_lo = 0x1C00,
		 uint32_t ram_hi = 0x4400, uint16_t khz = 250)
{
	Bytes b = {0x90, 0x09, mode, flags, static_cast<uint8_t>(khz), static_cast<uint8_t>(khz >> 8)};
	le32(b, ram_lo);
	le32(b, ram_hi);
	return b;
}

Bytes write_req(uint32_t addr, size_t nbytes)
{
	Bytes b = {0x90, 0x04};
	le32(b, addr);
	for (size_t i = 0; i < nbytes; ++i) {
		b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(i));
	}
	return b;
}

Bytes read_req(uint32_t addr, uint16_t nwords)
{
	Bytes b = {0x90, 0x03};
	le32(b, addr);
	b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(nwords));
	b.push_back(static_cast<uint8_t>(nwords >> 8));
	return b;
}

Bytes bp_req(uint8_t index, uint32_t addr)
{
	Bytes b = {0x90, 0x11, index};
	le32(b, addr);
	return b;
}

bool log_has(const FakeTap &t, const char *entry)
{
	for (const auto &l : t.log) {
		if (l == entry) {
			return true;
		}
	}
	return false;
}

struct Fx {
	FakeTap tap;
	msp430::Cpuxv2 cpu{tap};
	msp430::Debugger dbg{tap, cpu};
	msp430::Session s{cpu, dbg, 1016};

	Bytes run(const Bytes &req)
	{
		uint8_t resp[1024] = {};
		const size_t n = s.handle(req, resp);
		return {resp, resp + n};
	}

	/* The next sync finds a running CPU that the EEM stops at once. */
	void cpu_runs()
	{
		tap.eem_control_script = {0x0000, 0x0080};
		tap.captured = {0x0008, 0x0000};
	}

	/* DBG_ATTACH without reset; then forget the transcript. */
	void attach()
	{
		cpu_runs();
		REQUIRE(run(attach_req())[1] == 0x00);
		tap.log.clear();
		tap.mem_writes.clear();
	}
};

} // namespace

TEST(session_dbg_attach_returns_identity_pc_and_breakpoints)
{
	Fx f;
	f.cpu_runs();
	const Bytes want = {0x90, 0x00,
			    0x91, 0x06, 0x01, 0x00, 0x1A, 0x00, 0x00, 0x55, 0x29, /* identity */
			    0x20, 0x45, 0x00, 0x00,                               /* PC 0x4520 */
			    0x01,                                                 /* stopped on request */
			    0x08};                                                /* 8 breakpoints */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req()), want);
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
	const Bytes info = f.run({0x90, 0x00});
	REQUIRE(info.size() > 3u);
	CHECK_EQ(info[3], 3); /* INFO reports DebugHalted */
	CHECK(!log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01")); /* no reset */
}

TEST(session_dbg_attach_with_reset)
{
	Fx f;
	const Bytes r = f.run(attach_req(1));
	REQUIRE(r.size() == 17u);
	CHECK_EQ(r[1], 0x00);
	CHECK_EQ(r[11] | (r[12] << 8), 0x441A); /* reset vector */
	CHECK_EQ(r[15], 4);                      /* stop reason: reset */
	CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01"));
}

TEST(session_dbg_attach_validates_arguments)
{
	Fx f;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(2)), kBadArg);                          /* mode */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 1)), kBadArg);                       /* full mode: B2 */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 2)), kBadArg);                       /* unknown flag */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x1C01)), kBadArg);               /* odd RAM start */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x4400, 0x1C00)), kBadArg);       /* empty RAM range */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x1C00, 0x100002)), kBadArg);     /* past 1 MB */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x1C00, 0x4400, 99)), kBadArg);   /* speed */
	Bytes shortreq = attach_req();
	shortreq.pop_back();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(shortreq), kBadArg);
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);

	uint8_t small[16] = {}; /* 2 + 15 bytes needed */
	CHECK_EQ(f.s.handle(attach_req(), small), 2u);
	CHECK_EQ(small[1], 0x01);
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
}

TEST(session_dbg_attach_no_target_carries_the_id)
{
	Fx f;
	f.tap.jtag_id = 0xFF;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(attach_req()), (Bytes{0x90, 0x04, 0xFF}));
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
}

TEST(session_debug_ops_need_a_debug_session)
{
	Fx f;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0A, 0x00}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0B}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0E}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0F}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x04, 0, 0, 0, 0}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(0, 0x4400)), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12, 0xFF}), kBadState);
	f.tap.quick_words = {0x2955};
	REQUIRE(f.run({0x90, 0x01, 0xFA, 0x00, 0x00})[1] == 0x00); /* Phase A CONNECT */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0B}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0F}), kBadState);
}

TEST(session_dbg_run_status_halt)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kOk);
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugRunning);
	/* still running: state 0, no reason, no breakpoint, PC 0 */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0xFF, 0, 0, 0, 0}));
	f.cpu_runs();
	f.tap.eem_control_script.push_front(0x0000); /* the halt's own look first */
	f.tap.mab = 0x4ABC;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0B}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0xB8, 0x4A, 0x00, 0x00}));
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0x01, 0x01, 0xFF, 0xB8, 0x4A, 0, 0}));
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0B}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0xB8, 0x4A, 0x00, 0x00})); /* again: Ok */
}

TEST(session_dbg_status_reports_a_breakpoint_stop)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(5, 0x4600)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kOk);
	f.tap.eem_control_script = {0x0080, 0x0080};
	f.tap.captured = {0x0008, 0x0000};
	f.tap.mab = 0x4604;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0x01, 0x02, 0x05, 0x00, 0x46, 0, 0}));
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
}

TEST(session_dbg_halted_only_ops_answer_running)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kOk);
	f.tap.log.clear();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0E}), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0F}), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x04, 0, 0, 0, 0}), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(0, 0x4400)), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12, 0xFF}), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(read_req(0x2400, 1)), kRunning);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x2400, 2)), kRunning);
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugRunning);
}

TEST(session_dbg_registers)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	f.tap.captured = {0x43F0, 0x0000};
	const Bytes r = f.run({0x90, 0x0F});
	REQUIRE(r.size() == 2u + 64u);
	CHECK_EQ(r[1], 0x00);
	CHECK_EQ(r[2] | (r[3] << 8), 0x4520); /* R0 */
	CHECK_EQ(r[6] | (r[7] << 8), 0x43F0); /* R1 */
	CHECK_EQ(r[10], 0x08);                /* R2 */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x04, 0x34, 0x12, 0x0F, 0x00}), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x03, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00}), kBadArg); /* R3 */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x04, 0x00, 0x00, 0x10, 0x00}), kBadArg); /* 21 bits */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x10, 0x04, 0x00}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0F, 0x00}), kBadArg);
}

TEST(session_dbg_step_returns_the_new_pc)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	f.cpu_runs();
	f.tap.eem_control_script = {0x0080, 0x0080};
	f.tap.mab = 0x4526;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0E}), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0x22, 0x45, 0x00, 0x00}));
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
}

TEST(session_dbg_breakpoint_arguments)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(8, 0x4400)), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(0, 0x4401)), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x11, 0x00, 0x00, 0x44}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12, 0x08}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(bp_req(7, 0x24400 - 2)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12, 0x07}), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x12, 0xFF}), kOk);
}

TEST(session_dbg_read_mem_keeps_the_context)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	f.tap.memory[0x4400] = 0xBEEF;
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(read_req(0x4400, 1)), (Bytes{0x90, 0x00, 0xEF, 0xBE}));
	CHECK(!log_has(f.tap, "ir:c2")); /* not ReadMemQuick, which moves the PC */
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
}

TEST(session_dbg_write_mem_whitelist)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	/* allowed: RAM, USB RAM, peripherals outside the flash controller */
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x2400, 4)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x1C00, 2)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x43FE, 2)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x0200, 2)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x013E, 2)), kOk);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x0150, 2)), kOk);
	const size_t writes = f.tap.mem_writes.size();
	/* refused: main flash, information memory, BSL, vacant memory, the
	 * flash controller, the top of the address space, a write that runs
	 * off the end of RAM */
	for (uint32_t addr : {0x4400u, 0x1800u, 0x1980u, 0x1000u, 0x17FEu, 0x1A00u, 0x1B00u,
			      0x0140u, 0x0144u, 0x014Eu, 0xFFFEu, 0x24400u, 0xFFFFEu}) {
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(addr, 2)), kProtected);
	}
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x43FE, 4)), kProtected);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x013E, 4)), kProtected);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x0FFE, 4)), kProtected);
	CHECK_EQ(f.tap.mem_writes.size(), writes);
}

TEST(session_dbg_write_mem_ram_is_the_intersection_with_the_known_device)
{
	{
		Fx f; /* the host passes a narrower range: only that */
		f.cpu_runs();
		REQUIRE(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x2400, 0x3000))[1] == 0x00);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x2400, 2)), kOk);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x1C00, 2)), kProtected);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x3000, 2)), kProtected);
	}
	{
		Fx f; /* a wrong host table that covers flash cannot open it */
		f.cpu_runs();
		REQUIRE(f.run(attach_req(0, 0, 0x1C00, 0x24400))[1] == 0x00);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x43FE, 2)), kOk);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x4400, 2)), kProtected);
	}
	{
		Fx f; /* an unknown device: no RAM writes at all */
		f.cpu_runs();
		f.tap.memory[0x1A04] = 0x1234;
		REQUIRE(f.run(attach_req())[1] == 0x00);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x2400, 2)), kProtected);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(write_req(0x0200, 2)), kOk);
	}
}

TEST(session_flash_ops_are_locked_in_debug_states)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	Bytes erase = {0x90, 0x05, 0x02};
	le32(erase, 0x4400);
	le32(erase, 0x2400);
	Bytes begin = {0x90, 0x06};
	le32(begin, 0x4400);
	le32(begin, 2);
	le32(begin, 0x2400);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(erase), kProtected);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(begin), kProtected);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x07, 0x34, 0x12}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x08}), kBadState);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x02}), kBadState); /* DISCONNECT: use DBG_DETACH */
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C}), kOk);
	f.tap.log.clear();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(erase), kProtected);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run(begin), kProtected);
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
}

TEST(session_dbg_detach)
{
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0A, 0x00}), kOk);
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
		CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "ir:a8"));
		CHECK(!log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01")); /* restore and run, no reset */
		CHECK(f.tap.log.back() == "close");
	}
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0A, 0x01}), kOk);
		CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01")); /* reset and run */
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
	}
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0A, 0x02}), kBadArg);
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0A}), kBadArg);
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
	}
}

TEST(session_abort_detaches_a_debug_session)
{
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		f.s.abort();
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
		CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "ir:a8"));
		CHECK(!log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01"));
		CHECK(f.tap.log.back() == "close");
	}
	{
		Fx f; /* running: halted first, then restored and released */
		f.attach();
		REQUIRE(f.run({0x90, 0x0C})[1] == 0x00);
		f.tap.log.clear();
		f.cpu_runs();
		f.tap.eem_control_script.push_front(0x0000);
		f.s.abort();
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
		CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "dr16:1501"));
		CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "ir:a8"));
		CHECK(!log_has(f.tap, "dr16:0c01"));
		CHECK(f.tap.log.back() == "close");
	}
}

TEST(session_connect_ends_a_debug_session_first)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	f.tap.quick_words = {0x2955};
	REQUIRE(f.run({0x90, 0x01, 0xFA, 0x00, 0x00})[1] == 0x00);
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Connected);
	CHECK(log_has(f.tap, "ir:a8"));
}

TEST(session_target_error_in_a_debug_session_ends_it)
{
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		f.tap.cntrl_sig = 0x0000; /* the CPU is no longer under JTAG control */
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run(read_req(0x2400, 1)), kTargetError);
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
		CHECK(f.tap.log.back() == "close");
	}
	{
		Fx f;
		f.attach();
		REQUIRE(f.run({0x90, 0x0C})[1] == 0x00);
		f.tap.jtag_id = 0xFF; /* target lost while running */
		CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D}), kTargetError);
		CHECK(f.s.state() == State::Idle);
		CHECK(f.tap.log.back() == "close");
	}
}

TEST(session_dbg_fixed_size_requests)
{
	Fx f;
	f.attach();
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0B, 0x00}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0D, 0x00}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0E, 0x00}), kBadArg);
	CHECK_BYTES(f.run({0x90, 0x0C, 0x00}), kBadArg);
	CHECK(f.s.state() == State::DebugHalted);
	CHECK(f.tap.log.empty());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace the session header**

Replace the contents of `lib/msp430/include/msp430/session.hpp` with:

```cpp
#pragma once
/* Parses the MSP430 SBW vendor command and keeps the session state.
 * Never reads outside the request nor writes outside the response; malformed
 * input yields an error status.
 */
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>

#include "msp430/cpuxv2.hpp"
#include "msp430/debug.hpp"
#include "msp430/protocol.hpp"

namespace msp430 {

class Session {
public:
	/* max_data_bytes: the transport's packet size minus the longest header,
	 * kept even: WRITE_MEM has 2 + 4 header bytes, so a 1024-byte packet
	 * allows at most 1018. It must also satisfy 2 + max_data_bytes <=
	 * response capacity for READ_MEM. */
	Session(Cpuxv2 &cpu, Debugger &dbg, uint16_t max_data_bytes)
		: cpu_(cpu), dbg_(dbg), max_data_bytes_(max_data_bytes)
	{
	}

	/* `request` starts at the vendor command ID. Returns the response
	 * length, or 0 if the request is not addressed to this handler or the
	 * response buffer has fewer than 2 bytes. Fixed-size requests with
	 * missing or extra bytes are BAD_ARG. DISCONNECT in Idle is BAD_STATE
	 * (clients that disconnect as cleanup ignore it).
	 * A command that ends with Status::TargetError while a session is open
	 * closes the session (the target is in an unknown state and the pins are
	 * released); the host has to connect again. */
	size_t handle(std::span<const uint8_t> request, std::span<uint8_t> response);

	/* Close an open session (USB loss, SWD takeover). Safe when idle. Must
	 * be called on the thread that calls handle(). When a write is in
	 * progress it re-synchronizes and wipes the funclet RAM first, which
	 * takes a few hundred milliseconds. A debug session is detached with
	 * DetachAction::Run: the program continues where it stopped. */
	void abort();

	/* Callers that mirror the session state (LED, pin ownership) must read
	 * it after every handle(), because a TARGET_ERROR closes the session
	 * internally. */
	State state() const { return state_; }

private:
	using In = std::span<const uint8_t>;
	using Out = std::span<uint8_t>;

	Status op_info(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_connect(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_disconnect(In args);
	Status op_read_mem(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_write_mem(In args);
	Status op_erase(In args);
	Status op_flash_begin(In args);
	Status op_flash_data(In args);
	Status op_flash_end(In args);
	Status op_dbg_attach(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_dbg_detach(In args);
	Status op_dbg_halt(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_dbg_resume(In args);
	Status op_dbg_status(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_dbg_step(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_dbg_read_regs(In args, Out out, size_t &n);
	Status op_dbg_write_reg(In args);
	Status op_dbg_bp_set(In args);
	Status op_dbg_bp_clear(In args);

	bool debugging() const
	{
		return state_ == State::DebugHalted || state_ == State::DebugRunning;
	}
	/* Read-only debug sessions: may [addr, addr + nbytes) be written? */
	bool writable(uint32_t addr, uint32_t nbytes) const;

	Cpuxv2 &cpu_;
	Debugger &dbg_;
	uint16_t max_data_bytes_;
	State state_ = State::Idle;
	uint32_t flash_remaining_ = 0; /* words still expected by the funclet */
	uint32_t ram_lo_ = 0;          /* writable RAM in a debug session */
	uint32_t ram_hi_ = 0;
};

} // namespace msp430
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Replace the session implementation**

Replace the contents of `lib/msp430/src/session.cpp` with (the Phase A handlers are unchanged except where the rules above say otherwise: `abort`, `op_info`, `op_disconnect`, `op_read_mem`, `op_write_mem`, `op_erase`, `op_flash_begin`):

```cpp
#include "msp430/session.hpp"

#include "msp430/funclets.hpp"

namespace msp430 {

namespace {

uint16_t get16(std::span<const uint8_t> b, size_t off)
{
	return static_cast<uint16_t>(b[off] | (b[off + 1] << 8));
}

uint32_t get32(std::span<const uint8_t> b, size_t off)
{
	return static_cast<uint32_t>(b[off]) | (static_cast<uint32_t>(b[off + 1]) << 8) |
	       (static_cast<uint32_t>(b[off + 2]) << 16) |
	       (static_cast<uint32_t>(b[off + 3]) << 24);
}

void put16(std::span<uint8_t> b, size_t off, uint16_t v)
{
	b[off] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v);
	b[off + 1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(v >> 8);
}

void put32(std::span<uint8_t> b, size_t off, uint32_t v)
{
	put16(b, off, static_cast<uint16_t>(v));
	put16(b, off + 2, static_cast<uint16_t>(v >> 16));
}

/* Even address, and [addr, addr + 2 * nwords) inside the 20-bit space. */
bool range_ok(uint32_t addr, uint64_t nwords)
{
	return (addr & 1u) == 0 && addr < kAddressSpace &&
	       addr + 2u * nwords <= kAddressSpace;
}

/* Read-only debug sessions may write the peripheral space except the flash
 * controller, and RAM. */
constexpr uint32_t kPeripheralEnd = 0x1000;
constexpr uint32_t kFlashCtlStart = 0x0140; /* FCTL1..FCTL4 */
constexpr uint32_t kFlashCtlEnd = 0x0150;

/* RAM of the devices the probe knows, by device ID. A read-only session may
 * write only RAM that lies inside both this range and the one the host
 * passes with DBG_ATTACH, so a wrong host table cannot open flash. */
struct KnownRam {
	uint16_t device_id;
	uint32_t lo, hi;
};
constexpr KnownRam kKnownRam[] = {
	{0x2955, 0x1C00, 0x4400}, /* MSP430F5529: USB RAM + RAM */
};

} // namespace

size_t Session::handle(std::span<const uint8_t> request, std::span<uint8_t> response)
{
	if (request.empty() || request[0] != kVendorCmd || response.size() < 2) {
		return 0;
	}
	response[0] = kVendorCmd;
	if (request.size() < 2) {
		response[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(Status::BadArg);
		return 2;
	}

	const In args = request.subspan(2);
	const Out out = response.subspan(2);
	size_t n = 0;
	Status st = Status::UnknownOp;

	switch (static_cast<Op>(request[1])) {
	case Op::Info:
		st = op_info(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::Connect:
		st = op_connect(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::Disconnect:
		st = op_disconnect(args);
		break;
	case Op::ReadMem:
		st = op_read_mem(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::WriteMem:
		st = op_write_mem(args);
		break;
	case Op::Erase:
		st = op_erase(args);
		break;
	case Op::FlashBegin:
		st = op_flash_begin(args);
		break;
	case Op::FlashData:
		st = op_flash_data(args);
		break;
	case Op::FlashEnd:
		st = op_flash_end(args);
		break;
	case Op::DbgAttach:
		st = op_dbg_attach(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::DbgDetach:
		st = op_dbg_detach(args);
		break;
	case Op::DbgHalt:
		st = op_dbg_halt(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::DbgResume:
		st = op_dbg_resume(args);
		break;
	case Op::DbgStatus:
		st = op_dbg_status(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::DbgStep:
		st = op_dbg_step(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::DbgReadRegs:
		st = op_dbg_read_regs(args, out, n);
		break;
	case Op::DbgWriteReg:
		st = op_dbg_write_reg(args);
		break;
	case Op::DbgBpSet:
		st = op_dbg_bp_set(args);
		break;
	case Op::DbgBpClear:
		st = op_dbg_bp_clear(args);
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}

	/* The debugger may have stopped or lost the CPU during the command. */
	if (debugging()) {
		state_ = !dbg_.attached() ? State::Idle
			 : dbg_.running() ? State::DebugRunning
					  : State::DebugHalted;
	}
	/* The target is in an unknown state (lost synchronization). Close the
	 * session so the pins are released; the host must connect again. */
	if (st == Status::TargetError && state_ != State::Idle) {
		abort();
	}

	response[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(st);
	return 2 + n;
}

void Session::abort()
{
	if (state_ == State::Flashing) {
		cpu_.flash_end(false);
	}
	if (debugging()) {
		dbg_.detach(DetachAction::Run);
	} else if (state_ != State::Idle) {
		cpu_.disconnect();
	}
	state_ = State::Idle;
	flash_remaining_ = 0;
	ram_lo_ = ram_hi_ = 0;
}

bool Session::writable(uint32_t addr, uint32_t nbytes) const
{
	const uint64_t end = static_cast<uint64_t>(addr) + nbytes;
	const bool peripheral = end <= kPeripheralEnd &&
				(end <= kFlashCtlStart || addr >= kFlashCtlEnd);
	const bool ram = addr >= ram_lo_ && end <= ram_hi_;
	return peripheral || ram;
}

Status Session::op_info(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (!args.empty() || out.size() < 9) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	out[0] = kProtocolVersion;
	out[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(state_);
	put16(out, 2, max_data_bytes_);
	put32(out, 4, kFeatureCpuxv2Flash | kFeatureDebug);
	out[8] = static_cast<uint8_t>(kHwBreakpoints);
	n = 9;
	return Status::Ok;
}

Status Session::op_connect(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (args.size() != 3 || out.size() < 9) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint16_t speed_khz = get16(args, 0);
	const uint8_t mode = args[2];
	if (speed_khz < kMinSpeedKhz || speed_khz > kMaxSpeedKhz || mode > 1) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}

	abort(); /* a new CONNECT always starts from a clean entry */

	Identity id;
	const Status st = cpu_.connect(static_cast<EntryMode>(mode), speed_khz * 1000u, id);
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		state_ = State::Connected;
		out[0] = id.jtag_id;
		put16(out, 1, id.core_id);
		put32(out, 3, id.device_id_ptr);
		put16(out, 7, id.device_id);
		n = 9;
	} else if (st == Status::NoTarget) {
		out[0] = id.jtag_id;
		n = 1;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_disconnect(In args)
{
	if (state_ == State::Idle || debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState; /* a debug session ends with DBG_DETACH */
	}
	if (!args.empty()) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	abort();
	return Status::Ok;
}

Status Session::op_read_mem(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (state_ != State::Connected && state_ != State::DebugHalted) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (args.size() != 6) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint32_t addr = get32(args, 0);
	const size_t nwords = get16(args, 4);
	const size_t nbytes = 2 * nwords;
	if (nwords == 0 || !range_ok(addr, nwords) || nbytes > max_data_bytes_ ||
	    nbytes > out.size()) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const Status st = state_ == State::DebugHalted ? dbg_.read_mem(addr, nwords, out.data())
						       : cpu_.read_mem(addr, nwords, out.data());
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		n = nbytes;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_write_mem(In args)
{
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (state_ != State::Connected && state_ != State::DebugHalted) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (args.size() < 4) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint32_t addr = get32(args, 0);
	const In data = args.subspan(4);
	if (data.empty() || (data.size() & 1u) != 0 || data.size() > max_data_bytes_ ||
	    !range_ok(addr, data.size() / 2)) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugHalted) {
		if (!writable(addr, static_cast<uint32_t>(data.size()))) {
			return Status::Protected;
		}
		return dbg_.write_mem(addr, data.size() / 2, data.data());
	}
	return cpu_.write_mem(addr, data.size() / 2, data.data());
}

Status Session::op_erase(In args)
{
	if (debugging()) {
		return Status::Protected; /* no flash writes in a debug session */
	}
	if (state_ != State::Connected) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (args.size() != 9) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint8_t mode = args[0];
	const uint32_t addr = get32(args, 1);
	const uint32_t ram = get32(args, 5);
	if (mode > 2 || !range_ok(addr, 1) || !range_ok(ram, kFlashEraseWords)) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	return cpu_.erase(static_cast<EraseMode>(mode), addr, ram);
}

Status Session::op_flash_begin(In args)
{
	if (debugging()) {
		return Status::Protected;
	}
	if (state_ != State::Connected) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (args.size() != 12) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint32_t addr = get32(args, 0);
	const uint32_t nwords = get32(args, 4);
	const uint32_t ram = get32(args, 8);
	if (nwords == 0 || !range_ok(addr, nwords) || !range_ok(ram, kFlashWriteWords)) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const Status st = cpu_.flash_begin(addr, nwords, ram);
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		state_ = State::Flashing;
		flash_remaining_ = nwords;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_flash_data(In args)
{
	if (state_ != State::Flashing) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	const size_t nwords = args.size() / 2;
	if (args.empty() || (args.size() & 1u) != 0 || args.size() > max_data_bytes_ ||
	    nwords > flash_remaining_) {
		/* Protocol rule: any FLASH_DATA error ends the write (the
		 * funclet itself could have continued). */
		const Status cleanup = cpu_.flash_end(false);
		state_ = State::Connected;
		flash_remaining_ = 0;
		return cleanup == Status::TargetError ? cleanup : Status::BadArg;
	}
	const Status st = cpu_.flash_data(nwords, args.data());
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		flash_remaining_ -= static_cast<uint32_t>(nwords);
	} else {
		state_ = State::Connected; /* Cpuxv2 already cleaned up */
		flash_remaining_ = 0;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_flash_end(In args)
{
	if (state_ != State::Flashing) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (!args.empty()) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const bool complete = flash_remaining_ == 0;
	const Status st = cpu_.flash_end(complete);
	state_ = State::Connected;
	flash_remaining_ = 0;
	if (!complete && st == Status::Ok) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_attach(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (args.size() != 12 || out.size() < 15) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const uint8_t mode = args[0];
	const uint8_t flags = args[1];
	const uint16_t speed_khz = get16(args, 2);
	const uint32_t ram_lo = get32(args, 4);
	const uint32_t ram_hi = get32(args, 8);
	/* flags bit 0 (full mode) belongs to phase B2 and is refused here. */
	if (mode > 1 || flags != 0 || speed_khz < kMinSpeedKhz || speed_khz > kMaxSpeedKhz ||
	    (ram_lo & 1u) != 0 || ram_lo >= ram_hi || ram_hi > kAddressSpace) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}

	abort(); /* a new attach always starts from a clean entry */

	Identity id;
	const Status st = dbg_.attach(static_cast<AttachMode>(mode), speed_khz * 1000u, id);
	if (st == Status::NoTarget) {
		out[0] = id.jtag_id;
		n = 1;
	}
	if (st != Status::Ok) {
		return st;
	}
	state_ = State::DebugHalted;
	ram_lo_ = ram_hi_ = 0;
	for (const KnownRam &k : kKnownRam) {
		if (k.device_id == id.device_id) {
			ram_lo_ = ram_lo > k.lo ? ram_lo : k.lo;
			ram_hi_ = ram_hi < k.hi ? ram_hi : k.hi;
		}
	}
	if (ram_lo_ >= ram_hi_) {
		ram_lo_ = ram_hi_ = 0;
	}
	out[0] = id.jtag_id;
	put16(out, 1, id.core_id);
	put32(out, 3, id.device_id_ptr);
	put16(out, 7, id.device_id);
	put32(out, 9, dbg_.pc());
	out[13] = static_cast<uint8_t>(dbg_.stop_reason());
	out[14] = static_cast<uint8_t>(kHwBreakpoints);
	n = 15;
	return Status::Ok;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_detach(In args)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (args.size() != 1 || args[0] > 1) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	dbg_.detach(static_cast<DetachAction>(args[0]));
	state_ = State::Idle;
	ram_lo_ = ram_hi_ = 0;
	return Status::Ok;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_halt(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (!args.empty() || out.size() < 4) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const Status st = dbg_.halt();
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		put32(out, 0, dbg_.pc());
		n = 4;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_resume(In args)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (!args.empty()) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	return dbg_.resume();
}

Status Session::op_dbg_status(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (!args.empty() || out.size() < 7) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	bool halted = false;
	const Status st = dbg_.poll(halted);
	if (st != Status::Ok) {
		return st;
	}
	out[0] = halted ? 1 : 0;
	out[1] = static_cast<uint8_t>(halted ? dbg_.stop_reason() : StopReason::None);
	out[2] = halted ? dbg_.breakpoint_hit() : kNoBreakpoint;
	put32(out, 3, halted ? dbg_.pc() : 0);
	n = 7;
	return Status::Ok;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_step(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (!args.empty() || out.size() < 4) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	const Status st = dbg_.step();
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		put32(out, 0, dbg_.pc());
		n = 4;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_read_regs(In args, Out out, size_t &n)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (!args.empty() || out.size() < 64) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	uint32_t regs[16] = {};
	const Status st = dbg_.read_regs(regs);
	if (st == Status::Ok) {
		for (size_t i = 0; i < 16; ++i) {
			put32(out, 4 * i, regs[i]);
		}
		n = 64;
	}
	return st;
}

Status Session::op_dbg_write_reg(In args)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (args.size() != 5) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	return dbg_.write_reg(args[0], get32(args, 1));
}

Status Session::op_dbg_bp_set(In args)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (args.size() != 5) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	return dbg_.set_breakpoint(args[0], get32(args, 1));
}

Status Session::op_dbg_bp_clear(In args)
{
	if (!debugging()) {
		return Status::BadState;
	}
	if (state_ == State::DebugRunning) {
		return Status::Running;
	}
	if (args.size() != 1) {
		return Status::BadArg;
	}
	return dbg_.clear_breakpoint(args[0]);
}

} // namespace msp430
```

### Task 15: Device table: memory map and debug access rules

The host side of the read-only rules. The memory map is what GDB is told (Task 18): peripherals and RAM as `ram`, information memory, the device descriptor and main flash as `rom`; the bootloader area (0x1000–0x17FF) and vacant memory are absent, so GDB does not access them. Reads in the bootloader area are refused on top: on the user's board a read there returned changing data in Phase A, and the user's rule is not to read it again. Writes go only to RAM and to peripherals outside the flash controller, the same whitelist the probe enforces.

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/msp430/tests/test_devices.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/devices.py`

```python
F5529 = devices.MSP430F5529


class DeviceTableTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_memory_map_leaves_out_the_bsl_and_vacant_memory(self):
        self.assertEqual(F5529.memory_map(), [
            (0x0000, 0x1000, "ram"), (0x1800, 0x1A00, "rom"), (0x1A00, 0x1B00, "rom"),
            (0x1C00, 0x4400, "ram"), (0x4400, 0x24400, "rom")])

    def test_debug_readable(self):

        self.assertTrue(F5529.debug_readable(0x19FE, 4))  # info memory into the TLV
        self.assertTrue(F5529.debug_readable(0x43FE, 4))  # RAM into flash
        self.assertFalse(F5529.debug_readable(0x1000, 2))  # BSL
        self.assertFalse(F5529.debug_readable(0x0FFE, 4))  # peripherals into the BSL
        self.assertFalse(F5529.debug_readable(0x1B00, 2))  # vacant
        self.assertFalse(F5529.debug_readable(0x243FE, 4))  # past the end of flash

            self.assertTrue(F5529.debug_writable(addr, n), hex(addr))
        for addr, n in ((0x4400, 2), (0x43FE, 4), (0x1800, 2), (0x1000, 2), (0x1A00, 2),
```

```python
        return self.region(addr)[0]

    def memory_map(self):
        """The regions a read-only debug session may access, as GDB's memory map
        lists them: [(start, end, "ram" | "rom")], sorted. The BSL area and
        vacant memory are absent, so GDB does not access them."""
        return sorted([(*self.peripherals, "ram"), (*self.info, "rom"), (*self.tlv, "rom"),
                       (*self.ram, "ram"), (self.main_start, self.main_end, "rom")])

    def debug_readable(self, addr: int, length: int) -> bool:
        """Every byte of [addr, addr + length) lies in a memory-map region."""
        end = addr + length
        while addr < end:
            region = next((r for r in self.memory_map() if r[0] <= addr < r[1]), None)
            if region is None:
                return False
            addr = region[1]
        return True

    def debug_writable(self, addr: int, length: int) -> bool:
        """Read-only debug sessions write RAM, and peripheral registers other
        than the flash controller. Nothing else."""
        end = addr + length
        in_ram = self.ram[0] <= addr and end <= self.ram[1]
        in_peripherals = self.peripherals[0] <= addr and end <= self.peripherals[1]
        hits_fctl = addr < self.flash_controller[1] and end > self.flash_controller[0]
        return length > 0 and (in_ram or (in_peripherals and not hits_fctl))
```

### Task 16: `probe.py` client and the fake firmware's debug model

`probe.py` speaks protocol version 2: the debug operations, the statuses `PROTECTED` and `RUNNING`, and the ninth `INFO` byte. The flash flow accepts versions 1 and 2 (its commands did not change), so an old probe still flashes. The fake firmware gets a debug model that follows `msp430::Session`'s rules from Task 13 (state, then `RUNNING`, then size; the write whitelist with the RAM window narrowed to the F5529's RAM; flash operations `PROTECTED` in a debug session; `CONNECT` and `DBG_ATTACH` detach an open debug session), plus knobs for the GDB server tests: a CPU that runs for a number of status polls and then stops at a breakpoint, a target lost while running, register-write recording.

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/msp430/tests/test_probe_debug.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/tests/test_probe.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/tests/test_flasher.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/probe.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/flasher.py`
- Modify: `tools/msp430/tests/fake_firmware.py`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `tools/msp430/tests/test_probe_debug.py`:

```python
import os
import struct
import sys
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))

import probe as probe_mod  # noqa: E402
from fake_firmware import FakeFirmware  # noqa: E402
from probe import ProbeError, SbwProbe, StatusError  # noqa: E402


class DebugClientTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.fw = FakeFirmware()
        self.probe = SbwProbe(self.fw)

    def attach(self, mode=probe_mod.ATTACH_RUNNING):
        return self.probe.dbg_attach(mode, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400)

    def test_attach_returns_identity_pc_and_breakpoints(self):
        a = self.attach()
        self.assertEqual((a.identity.jtag_id, a.identity.device_id), (0x91, 0x2955))
        self.assertEqual((a.pc, a.stop_reason, a.hw_breakpoints),
                         (0x4520, probe_mod.STOP_REQUEST, 8))
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.attaches, [(0, 0, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400)])
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.state, probe_mod.STATE_DEBUG_HALTED)

    def test_attach_with_reset(self):
        a = self.attach(probe_mod.ATTACH_RESET)
        self.assertEqual((a.pc, a.stop_reason), (0x441A, probe_mod.STOP_RESET))

    def test_attach_failure_carries_the_status(self):
        self.fw.attach_status = probe_mod.STATUS_NO_TARGET
        with self.assertRaises(StatusError) as cm:
            self.attach()
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.status, probe_mod.STATUS_NO_TARGET)
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.data, b"\xFF")

    def test_run_poll_halt(self):
        self.attach()
        self.probe.dbg_resume()
        self.assertEqual(self.probe.dbg_status(), probe_mod.DebugStatus(False, 0, 0xFF, 0))
        self.assertEqual(self.probe.dbg_halt(), 0x4ABC)
        st = self.probe.dbg_status()
        self.assertEqual((st.halted, st.stop_reason, st.pc), (True, probe_mod.STOP_REQUEST, 0x4ABC))

    def test_breakpoint_stop(self):
        self.attach()
        self.probe.dbg_bp_set(2, 0x4600)
        self.probe.dbg_resume()
        self.assertFalse(self.probe.dbg_status().halted)
        st = self.probe.dbg_status()
        self.assertEqual(st, probe_mod.DebugStatus(True, probe_mod.STOP_BREAKPOINT, 2, 0x4600))
        self.probe.dbg_bp_clear(2)
        self.probe.dbg_bp_clear()
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.breakpoints, {})

    def test_registers(self):
        self.attach()
        regs = self.probe.dbg_read_regs()
        self.assertEqual(len(regs), 16)
        self.assertEqual(regs[:3], [0x4520, 0x43F0, 0x0008])
        self.probe.dbg_write_reg(12, 0xA5432)
        self.assertEqual(self.probe.dbg_read_regs()[12], 0xA5432)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(StatusError, "DBG_WRITE_REG failed: BAD_ARG"):
            self.probe.dbg_write_reg(3, 0)

    def test_step(self):
        self.attach()
        self.assertEqual(self.probe.dbg_step(), 0x4522)

    def test_running_and_protected_statuses_name_themselves(self):
        self.attach()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(StatusError, "WRITE_MEM failed: PROTECTED"):
            self.probe.write_mem(0x4400, b"\x00\x00")
        self.probe.dbg_resume()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(StatusError, "DBG_READ_REGS failed: RUNNING"):
            self.probe.dbg_read_regs()

    def test_memory_in_a_debug_session(self):
        self.attach()
        self.probe.write_mem(0x2400, b"\x34\x12")
        self.probe.write_mem(0x0200, b"\x01\x00")
        self.assertEqual(self.probe.read_mem(0x2400, 1), b"\x34\x12")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.mem[0x0200], 1)

    def test_detach(self):
        self.attach()
        self.probe.dbg_detach()
        self.assertEqual((self.fw.detaches, self.fw.state), ([0], 0))
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(StatusError, "DBG_DETACH failed: BAD_STATE"):
            self.probe.dbg_detach()

    def test_short_replies_are_out_of_step(self):
        self.attach()
        self.fw.transfer = lambda req, timeout_ms=3000: bytes([0x90, 0x00])
        for call in (self.probe.dbg_halt, self.probe.dbg_status, self.probe.dbg_step,
                     self.probe.dbg_read_regs):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ProbeError, "out of step"):
                call()


class FakeFirmwareDebugRulesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The fake follows msp430::Session's debug rules."""

    def cmd(self, fw, op, args=b""):
        return fw.transfer(bytes([0x90, op]) + args)

    def attached(self, lo=0x1C00, hi=0x4400):
        fw = FakeFirmware()
        SbwProbe(fw).dbg_attach(0, 250, lo, hi)
        return fw

    def test_attach_argument_rules(self):
        fw = FakeFirmware()
        pk = struct.pack
        for args in (pk("<BBHII", 2, 0, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400), pk("<BBHII", 0, 1, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400),
                     pk("<BBHII", 0, 0, 99, 0x1C00, 0x4400), pk("<BBHII", 0, 0, 250, 0x1C01, 0x4400),
                     pk("<BBHII", 0, 0, 250, 0x4400, 0x1C00), pk("<BBHII", 0, 0, 250, 0, 0x100002),
                     pk("<BBHII", 0, 0, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400)[:-1]):
            self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 9, args), bytes([0x90, 1]), args)
        self.assertEqual(fw.attaches, [])

    def test_state_running_then_size(self):
        fw = FakeFirmware()
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 15), bytes([0x90, 2]))
        fw = self.attached()
        self.cmd(fw, 12)
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 15, b"\x00"), bytes([0x90, 10]))
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 13, b"\x00"), bytes([0x90, 1]))

    def test_flash_ops_and_disconnect_in_a_debug_session(self):
        fw = self.attached()
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 5, struct.pack("<BII", 2, 0x4400, 0x2400)), bytes([0x90, 9]))
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 6, struct.pack("<III", 0x4400, 1, 0x2400)), bytes([0x90, 9]))
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 2), bytes([0x90, 2]))

    def test_ram_is_narrowed_to_the_device(self):
        fw = self.attached(0x1C00, 0x24400)
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 4, struct.pack("<I", 0x43FE) + b"\0\0"), bytes([0x90, 0]))
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 4, struct.pack("<I", 0x4400) + b"\0\0"), bytes([0x90, 9]))

    def test_connect_detaches_a_debug_session(self):
        fw = self.attached()
        SbwProbe(fw).connect(250, 0)
        self.assertEqual((fw.detaches, fw.state), ([0], 1))

    def test_lost_target_while_running(self):
        fw = self.attached()
        self.cmd(fw, 12)
        fw.lost_on_poll = True
        self.assertEqual(self.cmd(fw, 13), bytes([0x90, 6]))
        self.assertEqual(fw.state, 0)
```


with:

```python
    def flash_end(self) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_FLASH_END)

    # --- debugging (protocol version 2) ---

    def dbg_attach(self, mode: int, speed_khz: int, ram_lo: int, ram_hi: int) -> Attached:
        """Attach without reset (ATTACH_RUNNING) or reset and halt (ATTACH_RESET).
        ram_lo/ram_hi: the RAM a read-only session may write (the probe narrows
        it to the RAM it knows for the device)."""
        data = self._cmd(OP_DBG_ATTACH, struct.pack("<BBHII", mode, 0, speed_khz, ram_lo, ram_hi))
        if len(data) < 15:
            raise ProbeError(OUT_OF_STEP)
        identity = Identity(*struct.unpack("<BHIH", data[:9]))
        pc, reason, nbp = struct.unpack("<IBB", data[9:15])
        return Attached(identity, pc, reason, nbp)

    def dbg_detach(self, action: int = DETACH_RUN) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_DBG_DETACH, bytes([action]))

    def dbg_halt(self) -> int:
        return self._u32(self._cmd(OP_DBG_HALT), OP_DBG_HALT)

    def dbg_resume(self) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_DBG_RESUME)

    def dbg_status(self) -> DebugStatus:
        data = self._cmd(OP_DBG_STATUS)
        if len(data) < 7:
            raise ProbeError(OUT_OF_STEP)
        state, reason, bp, pc = struct.unpack("<BBBI", data[:7])
        return DebugStatus(state == 1, reason, bp, pc)

    def dbg_step(self) -> int:
        return self._u32(self._cmd(OP_DBG_STEP), OP_DBG_STEP)

    def dbg_read_regs(self) -> list:
        data = self._cmd(OP_DBG_READ_REGS)
        if len(data) < 64:
            raise ProbeError(OUT_OF_STEP)
        return list(struct.unpack("<16I", data[:64]))

    def dbg_write_reg(self, index: int, value: int) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_DBG_WRITE_REG, struct.pack("<BI", index, value))

    def dbg_bp_set(self, index: int, addr: int) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_DBG_BP_SET, struct.pack("<BI", index, addr))

    def dbg_bp_clear(self, index: int = NO_BREAKPOINT) -> None:
        self._cmd(OP_DBG_BP_CLEAR, bytes([index]))

    @staticmethod
    def _u32(data: bytes, op: int) -> int:
        if len(data) < 4:
            raise ProbeError(OUT_OF_STEP)
        return struct.unpack("<I", data[:4])[0]
```

Replace:

```python
Fault injection knobs: version, features, connect_status, fail_op,
interrupt_on_op, fail_flash_after, stuck, no_target, has_handler, also_erase,
skip_segment_erases, read_shift."""
import struct

OK, BAD_ARG, BAD_STATE, BUSY, NO_TARGET, LOCKED, TARGET_ERROR, TIMEOUT, UNKNOWN_OP = range(9)
MAIN = (0x4400, 0x24400)
RAM = (0x2400, 0x4400)

# Argument bytes of the fixed-size requests; the firmware rejects any other length.
FIXED_ARGS = {0: 0, 1: 3, 2: 0, 3: 6, 5: 9, 6: 12, 8: 0}

ADDR_LIMIT = 0x100000    # 20-bit address space
```

with:

```python
Fault injection knobs: version, features, connect_status, fail_op,
interrupt_on_op, fail_flash_after, stuck, no_target, has_handler, also_erase,
skip_segment_erases, read_shift; for debugging: attach_status, run_polls,
stop_at, halt_pc, step_size, lost_on_poll.

Debug model (ops 9-18, states 3 DebugHalted / 4 DebugRunning): sixteen
registers, up to eight hardware breakpoints, and a CPU that, once resumed,
runs for `run_polls` DBG_STATUS polls and then stops at `stop_at`, or at the
breakpoint with the lowest index if `stop_at` is None; with neither it runs
until DBG_HALT. In a debug session WRITE_MEM writes only peripherals outside
the flash controller and RAM (the host's range narrowed to the F5529's
0x1C00-0x43FF) and answers PROTECTED elsewhere, as the firmware does."""
import struct

OK, BAD_ARG, BAD_STATE, BUSY, NO_TARGET, LOCKED, TARGET_ERROR, TIMEOUT, UNKNOWN_OP = range(9)
PROTECTED, RUNNING = 9, 10
MAIN = (0x4400, 0x24400)
RAM = (0x2400, 0x4400)
DEBUG_RAM = (0x1C00, 0x4400)  # what the firmware knows for the F5529
PERIPHERALS = (0x0000, 0x1000)
FCTL = (0x0140, 0x0150)
HALTED, RUNNING_STATE = 3, 4

# Argument bytes of the fixed-size requests; the firmware rejects any other length.
FIXED_ARGS = {0: 0, 1: 3, 2: 0, 3: 6, 5: 9, 6: 12, 8: 0,
              9: 12, 10: 1, 11: 0, 12: 0, 13: 0, 14: 0, 15: 0, 16: 5, 17: 5, 18: 1}

ADDR_LIMIT = 0x100000    # 20-bit address space
```

```python
        return status, b""

    def _abort(self):
        """Session::abort(): a debug session is detached with action 0."""
        if self.state in (HALTED, RUNNING_STATE):
            self.detaches.append(0)
            self.breakpoints = {}
        self.state = 0

    def _debug_writable(self, addr, n):
        end = addr + n
        peripheral = end <= PERIPHERALS[1] and (end <= FCTL[0] or addr >= FCTL[1])
        ram = self.ram_range[0] <= addr and end <= self.ram_range[1]
        return peripheral or ram

    def _halted_check(self, op, a):
        """State, then RUNNING, then the fixed size: the firmware's order."""
        if self.state not in (HALTED, RUNNING_STATE):
            return BAD_STATE
        if self.state == RUNNING_STATE and op != 13 and op != 11 and op != 10:
            return RUNNING
        if op in FIXED_ARGS and len(a) != FIXED_ARGS[op]:
            return BAD_ARG
        return None

    def _op(self, op, a):
        bad_len = op in FIXED_ARGS and len(a) != FIXED_ARGS[op]
```

```python
    def _dbg_attach(self, a):
        if len(a) != 12:
            return BAD_ARG, b""
        mode, flags, speed, lo, hi = struct.unpack("<BBHII", a)
        if mode > 1 or flags != 0 or not 100 <= speed <= 1000 or lo & 1 or lo >= hi or hi > ADDR_LIMIT:
            return BAD_ARG, b""
        self.attaches.append((mode, flags, speed, lo, hi))
        self._abort()
        if self.attach_status is not None:
            return self.attach_status, (bytes([self.no_target_id]) if self.attach_status == NO_TARGET else b"")
        if 9 in self.fail_op:
            return self._fail(9)
        self.state = HALTED
        self.breakpoints = {}
        self.ram_range = (max(lo, DEBUG_RAM[0]), min(hi, DEBUG_RAM[1]))
        if mode == 1:
            self.regs[0], self.regs[2] = self.reset_pc, 0
        self.stop_reason, self.bp_hit = (4 if mode == 1 else 1), 0xFF
        identity = struct.pack("<BHIH", 0x91, 0x0106, 0x1A00, self.device_id)
        return OK, identity + struct.pack("<IBB", self.regs[0], self.stop_reason, 8)

    def _stop(self, pc, reason):
        self.state = HALTED
        self.regs[0] = pc
        self.stop_reason = reason
        self.bp_hit = next((i for i, x in sorted(self.breakpoints.items()) if x == pc), 0xFF)

    def _dbg_op(self, op, a):
        if op == 10:  # DBG_DETACH
            if a[0] > 1:
                return BAD_ARG, b""
            self.detaches.append(a[0])
            self.breakpoints = {}
            self.state = 0
            return OK, b""
        if op == 11:  # DBG_HALT
            if self.state == RUNNING_STATE:
                self._stop(self.halt_pc, 1)
            return OK, struct.pack("<I", self.regs[0])
        if op == 12:  # DBG_RESUME
            self.state = RUNNING_STATE
            self.resumes += 1
            self._polls = 0
            return OK, b""
        if op == 13:  # DBG_STATUS
            if self.state == RUNNING_STATE:
                if self.lost_on_poll:
                    self.state = 0
                    return TARGET_ERROR, b""
                self._polls += 1
                target = self.stop_at
                if target is None and self.breakpoints:
                    target = self.breakpoints[min(self.breakpoints)]
                if target is not None and self._polls >= self.run_polls:
                    self._stop(target, 2)
            if self.state == RUNNING_STATE:
                return OK, struct.pack("<BBBI", 0, 0, 0xFF, 0)
            return OK, struct.pack("<BBBI", 1, self.stop_reason, self.bp_hit, self.regs[0])
        if op == 14:  # DBG_STEP
            self.steps += 1
            self._stop(self.regs[0] + self.step_size, 3)
            return OK, struct.pack("<I", self.regs[0])
        if op == 15:  # DBG_READ_REGS
            return OK, struct.pack("<16I", *self.regs)
        if op == 16:  # DBG_WRITE_REG
            index, value = struct.unpack("<BI", a)
            if (index > 15 or index == 3 or value >= ADDR_LIMIT or (index == 0 and value & 1)
                    or (index == 2 and value > 0xFFFF)):
                return BAD_ARG, b""
            self.regs[index] = value
            self.reg_writes.append((index, value))
            return OK, b""
        if op == 17:  # DBG_BP_SET
            index, addr = struct.unpack("<BI", a)
            if index >= 8 or addr & 1 or addr >= ADDR_LIMIT:
                return BAD_ARG, b""
            self.breakpoints[index] = addr
            return OK, b""
        # op == 18, DBG_BP_CLEAR
        if a[0] == 0xFF:
            self.breakpoints = {}
        elif a[0] >= 8:
            return BAD_ARG, b""
        else:
            self.breakpoints.pop(a[0], None)
        return OK, b""
```

```python
class FramingTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_frame_and_checksum(self):
        self.assertEqual(rsp.frame(b"OK"), b"$OK#9a")
        self.assertEqual(rsp.frame(b""), b"$#00")

    def test_escape_round_trip(self):
        data = bytes(range(256))
        escaped = rsp.escape(data)
        for special in b"#$*":
            self.assertNotIn(special, escaped)
        self.assertEqual(rsp.unescape(escaped), data)
        self.assertEqual(rsp.escape(b"}"), b"}]")


class PacketReaderTest(unittest.TestCase):

        events = r.feed(b"+$g#67\x03-$m0,2#fb")
        self.assertEqual(events, [("ack", None), ("packet", b"g"), ("interrupt", None),
                                  ("nack", None), ("packet", b"m0,2")])

    def test_split_packet_waits_for_the_rest(self):
        r = rsp.PacketReader()
        self.assertEqual(r.feed(b"$qSupp"), [])
        self.assertEqual(r.feed(b"orted#"), [])
        self.assertEqual(r.feed(b"37"), [("packet", b"qSupported")])

        self.assertEqual(r.feed(b"$g#00$g#67"), [("bad", None), ("packet", b"g")])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the framing module**

Create `tools/msp430/rsp.py`:

```python
"""GDB Remote Serial Protocol framing: packets, acknowledgements, interrupts.

    packet:    $<payload>#<two hex digits: sum of the payload bytes mod 256>
    ack/nack:  + / -
    interrupt: the single byte 0x03 (Ctrl-C in GDB)

Binary payloads (the X packet, qXfer replies) escape '#', '$', '}' and '*'
as '}' followed by the byte XOR 0x20.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

INTERRUPT = 0x03
_SPECIAL = (0x23, 0x24, 0x2A, 0x7D)  # '#', '$', '*', '}'


def checksum(payload: bytes) -> int:
    return sum(payload) & 0xFF


def frame(payload: bytes) -> bytes:
    return b"$" + payload + b"#" + b"%02x" % checksum(payload)


def escape(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    for b in data:
        if b in _SPECIAL:
            out += bytes([0x7D, b ^ 0x20])
        else:
            out.append(b)
    return bytes(out)


def unescape(data: bytes) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    it = iter(data)
    for b in it:
        if b == 0x7D:
            out.append(next(it, 0x20) ^ 0x20)
        else:
            out.append(b)
    return bytes(out)


class PacketReader:
    """Splits the bytes GDB sends into events:
    ("packet", payload), ("bad", None) for a packet with a wrong checksum,
    ("interrupt", None), ("ack", None) and ("nack", None). Other bytes between
    packets are dropped. An incomplete packet waits for more data."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._buf = bytearray()

    def feed(self, data: bytes) -> list:
        self._buf += data
        events = []
        while self._buf:
            c = self._buf[0]
            if c == ord("$"):
                end = self._buf.find(b"#")
                if end < 0 or len(self._buf) < end + 3:
                    break
                payload = bytes(self._buf[1:end])
                try:
                    want = int(self._buf[end + 1:end + 3], 16)
                except ValueError:
                    want = -1
                del self._buf[:end + 3]
                events.append(("packet", payload) if want == checksum(payload) else ("bad", None))
            else:
                del self._buf[0]
                if c == INTERRUPT:
                    events.append(("interrupt", None))
                elif c == ord("+"):
                    events.append(("ack", None))
                elif c == ord("-"):
                    events.append(("nack", None))
        return events
```

### Task 18: `gdbserver.py`

The GDB server in read-only mode. Decisions behind the code:

- **Packets**: `qSupported` (`PacketSize=1000` hex, `qXfer:memory-map:read+`, `swbreak+`, `hwbreak+`), `?`, `g`/`G`, `p`/`P`, `m`/`M`/`X`, `c`/`s` (with an optional address), `vCont?`/`vCont;c|s`, `Z1`/`z1`, `Z0`/`z0`, `vFlash*`, `D`, `k`/`vKill`, `qRcmd` (monitor), `qXfer:memory-map:read`, `qAttached` (`1`: GDB detaches rather than kills on quit), `H…`, `qSymbol::`; anything else gets the empty reply ("not supported").
- **No target description** (see "Read first"): `qXfer:features:read` is not offered; `g` is 16 little-endian 32-bit words.
- **Stop replies** carry the PC as expedited register 0: `T05` after attach, a step or a breakpoint (with `hwbreak:;`), `T02` after an interrupt (0x03). Reporting `swbreak+`/`hwbreak+` keeps GDB from applying `decr_pc_after_break` (1 in `msp430-tdep.c`) to a hardware-breakpoint stop.
- **Read-only refusals happen before the probe is asked**: `M`/`X` outside RAM and peripherals (flash controller excluded), `Z0` (answered `E01`, not empty, so GDB does not fall back to writing a breakpoint instruction into memory), `vFlash*` (`load`). Reads outside the memory map or in the bootloader area are refused too. Every refusal prints `refused: ...` on the server's console; GDB shows its own error.
- **Running**: after `c` the server polls `DBG_STATUS` every 50 ms; 0x03 sends `DBG_HALT`. Packets that arrive while the target runs are acknowledged and answered after the stop. Bytes GDB sends in one segment (`$c#63` followed by 0x03) are queued in order, so an interrupt is never lost.
- **Leaving**: `D`, `k`, a dropped connection, Ctrl-C and any other exit from the connection detach with action 0 (restore and run). A `TARGET_ERROR` ends the session: during a run the server sends a console message and `W00`, otherwise `E03`, and closes the connection.
- **Attach**: when GDB connects, with up to four tries of the normal entry (no magic pattern: that would reset the target), the device ID checked against the device table (a wrong device is detached at once and left running).
- **Byte writes** are word read-modify-writes. **Bind address** is 127.0.0.1 only.

The tests drive the server through one end of a socket pair with a scripted GDB client; the server runs in a thread on the other end, against `FakeFirmware`. Create `tools/msp430/tests/test_gdbserver.py`:

```python
import io
import os
import socket
import struct
import sys
import threading
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))

import devices  # noqa: E402
import gdbserver  # noqa: E402
import probe as probe_mod  # noqa: E402
import rsp  # noqa: E402
from fake_firmware import NO_TARGET, FakeFirmware  # noqa: E402
from probe import ProbeError, SbwProbe  # noqa: E402


class Client:
    """A scripted GDB: sends packets, acknowledges and returns the replies."""

    def __init__(self, sock):
        self.sock = sock
        self.sock.settimeout(5)
        self.reader = rsp.PacketReader()
        self.events = []
        self.raw = []  # every event seen

    def _event(self):
        while not self.events:
            data = self.sock.recv(4096)
            if not data:
                return ("closed", None)
            self.events += self.reader.feed(data)
        ev = self.events.pop(0)
        self.raw.append(ev)
        return ev

    def reply(self):
        """The next packet from the server (acknowledged); O packets are collected."""
        while True:
            kind, payload = self._event()
            if kind == "closed":
                return None
            if kind == "packet":
                try:
                    self.sock.sendall(b"+")
                except OSError:
                    pass  # the server has already closed the connection
                return payload.decode("latin-1")

    def cmd(self, payload):
        if isinstance(payload, str):
            payload = payload.encode("latin-1")
        self.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(payload))
        return self.reply()


class ServerFixture(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.fw = FakeFirmware()
        self.out = io.StringIO()
        self.server = gdbserver.GdbServer(SbwProbe(self.fw), devices.MSP430F5529,
                                          out=self.out, poll_interval=0.001)
        self.server_sock, client_sock = socket.socketpair()
        self.gdb = Client(client_sock)
        self.error = None
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._serve, daemon=True)

    def _serve(self):
        try:
            self.server.serve_connection(self.server_sock)
        except ProbeError as e:
            self.error = e
        finally:
            self.server_sock.close()

    def start(self):
        self.thread.start()

    def finish(self):
        self.gdb.sock.close()
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertFalse(self.thread.is_alive())

    def tearDown(self):
        if self.thread.is_alive():
            self.finish()


class ConnectionTest(ServerFixture):
    def test_attach_without_reset_and_stop_reply(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("qSupported:multiprocess+;swbreak+;hwbreak+"),
                         "PacketSize=1000;qXfer:memory-map:read+;swbreak+;hwbreak+")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("?"), "T0500:20450000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.attaches, [(0, 0, 250, 0x1C00, 0x4400)])
        self.assertIn("halted at 0x04520; read-only mode; 8 hardware breakpoints", self.out.getvalue())

    def test_attach_with_reset(self):
        self.server.attach_mode = probe_mod.ATTACH_RESET
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("?"), "T0500:1a440000;")

    def test_unknown_packets_get_an_empty_reply(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("qTStatus"), "")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vMustReplyEmpty"), "")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Hg0"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("qAttached"), "1")

    def test_bad_checksum_gets_a_nack(self):
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(b"$g#00")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb._event(), ("nack", None))

    def test_attach_failure(self):
        self.fw.attach_status = NO_TARGET
        self.start()
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertIn("no MSP430 found (JTAG ID read as 0xFF)", str(self.error))
        self.assertEqual(len(self.fw.attaches), 4)
        self.assertIsNone(self.gdb.reply())  # connection closed

    def test_wrong_device_is_left_running(self):
        self.fw.device_id = 0x1234
        self.start()
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertIn("unexpected device", str(self.error))
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [0])


class RegisterTest(ServerFixture):
    def test_g_is_sixteen_little_endian_words(self):
        self.start()
        g = self.gdb.cmd("g")
        self.assertEqual(len(g), 128)
        self.assertEqual(list(struct.unpack("<16I", bytes.fromhex(g))), self.fw.regs)

    def test_p_and_P(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("p1"), "f0430000")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Pc=3254" + "0a00"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.regs[12], 0xA5432)
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("pc"), "32540a00")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("P3=00000000"), "E01")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("p10"), "E01")

    def test_G_writes_only_what_changed(self):
        self.start()
        regs = list(self.fw.regs)
        regs[5] = 0x1234
        regs[3] = 0x9999  # CG is never written
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("G" + struct.pack("<16I", *regs).hex()), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.regs[5], 0x1234)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.regs[3], 0)


class MemoryTest(ServerFixture):
    def test_read_flash_ram_and_unaligned(self):
        self.fw.mem.update({0x4400: 0x11, 0x4401: 0x22, 0x4402: 0x33, 0x2400: 0xAA})
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("m4400,3"), "112233")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("m4401,1"), "22")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("m2400,1"), "aa")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("m4400,0"), "")

    def test_bsl_and_vacant_memory_are_never_read(self):
        self.start()
        chunks = list(self.fw.read_chunks)
        for packet in ("m1000,2", "m17f0,10", "mffe,4", "m1b00,2", "m24400,2", "mffffe,4"):
            self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd(packet), "E01", packet)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.read_chunks, chunks)
        self.assertIn("the BSL area 0x1000-0x17FF is never read", self.out.getvalue())

    def test_write_ram_and_peripherals(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("M2400,2:3412"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual((self.fw.mem[0x2400], self.fw.mem[0x2401]), (0x34, 0x12))
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("M2403,1:77"), "OK")  # odd: read-modify-write
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.mem[0x2403], 0x77)
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd(b"X2404,2:" + rsp.escape(b"#}")), "OK")
        self.assertEqual((self.fw.mem[0x2404], self.fw.mem[0x2405]), (0x23, 0x7D))
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("X2400,0:"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("M0200,2:0100"), "OK")

    def test_writes_outside_ram_and_peripherals_are_refused_before_the_probe(self):
        self.start()
        writes = list(self.fw.write_chunks)
        for packet in ("M4400,2:0000", "M1800,2:0000", "M1000,2:0000", "M0140,2:0000",
                       "M43fe,4:00000000", "Mfffe,2:0000", "X4400,2:\x00\x00"):
            self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd(packet), "E01", packet)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.write_chunks, writes)
        self.assertIn("read-only mode writes only RAM and peripheral registers", self.out.getvalue())

    def test_flash_programming_is_refused(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vFlashErase:4400,200"), "E01")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vFlashWrite:4400:\x00\x00"), "E01")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vFlashDone"), "E01")
        self.assertNotIn(("erase", 0, 0x4400, 0x2400), self.fw.ops)

    def test_memory_map(self):
        self.start()
        whole = self.gdb.cmd("qXfer:memory-map:read::0,1000")
        self.assertTrue(whole.startswith("l<?xml"))
        self.assertIn('<memory type="ram" start="0x1c00" length="0x2800"/>', whole)
        self.assertIn('<memory type="rom" start="0x4400" length="0x20000"/>', whole)
        self.assertNotIn('start="0x1000"', whole)
        first = self.gdb.cmd("qXfer:memory-map:read::0,20")
        self.assertEqual(first[0], "m")
        self.assertEqual(first[1:], whole[1:0x21])


class RunControlTest(ServerFixture):
    def test_hardware_breakpoint_and_continue(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,4600,2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.breakpoints, {0: 0x4600})
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("c"), "T05hwbreak:;00:00460000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.state, probe_mod.STATE_DEBUG_HALTED)
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("z1,4600,2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.breakpoints, {})

    def test_software_breakpoints_are_refused(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z0,2400,2"), "E01")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("z0,2400,2"), "OK")
        self.assertIn("software breakpoints are not allowed", self.out.getvalue())
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.write_chunks, [])

    def test_breakpoint_slots(self):
        self.start()
        for i in range(8):
            self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd(f"Z1,{0x4600 + 2 * i:x},2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,4600,2"), "OK")  # already set: no new slot
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,5000,2"), "E01")
        self.assertIn("all 8 hardware breakpoints are in use", self.out.getvalue())
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("z1,4602,2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,5000,2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.breakpoints[1], 0x5000)

    def test_interrupt_halts(self):
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"vCont;c"))
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(b"\x03")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.reply(), "T0200:bc4a0000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.state, probe_mod.STATE_DEBUG_HALTED)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.fw.resumes, 1)

    def test_interrupt_in_the_same_read_as_the_continue(self):
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"c") + b"\x03")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.reply(), "T0200:bc4a0000;")

    def test_packet_sent_while_running_is_answered_after_the_stop(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,4600,2"), "OK")
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"c") + rsp.frame(b"qAttached"))
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.reply(), "T05hwbreak:;00:00460000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.reply(), "1")

    def test_step(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vCont?"), "vCont;c;C;s;S")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("s"), "T0500:22450000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("vCont;s:1"), "T0500:24450000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.steps, 2)

    def test_continue_from_an_address(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("Z1,4700,2"), "OK")
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("c4500"), "T05hwbreak:;00:00470000;")
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.reg_writes, [(0, 0x4500)])
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.resumes, 1)

    def test_lost_target_while_running(self):
        self.fw.lost_on_poll = True
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"c"))
        replies = [self.gdb.reply(), self.gdb.reply()]
        self.assertTrue(replies[0].startswith("O"))
        self.assertIn("target lost", bytes.fromhex(replies[0][1:]).decode())
        self.assertEqual(replies[1], "W00")
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [])  # nothing left to detach

    def test_target_error_on_a_command_ends_the_session(self):
        self.start()
        self.fw.fail_op = {3: 6}
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("m4400,2"), "E03")
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertFalse(self.thread.is_alive())


class DetachTest(ServerFixture):
    def test_D_detaches_and_ends_the_session(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("D"), "OK")
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [0])
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.state, 0)
        self.assertIn("the program continues where it stopped", self.out.getvalue())

    def test_k_detaches_too(self):
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"k"))
        self.thread.join(5)
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [0])

    def test_dropped_connection_detaches(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("?"), "T0500:20450000;")
        self.finish()
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [0])

    def test_dropped_connection_while_running_detaches(self):
        self.start()
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"c"))
        self.gdb._event()  # the ack: the server is running the target
        self.finish()
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.detaches, [0])
        self.assertEqual(self.fw.state, 0)


class MonitorTest(ServerFixture):
    def monitor(self, text):
        self.gdb.sock.sendall(rsp.frame(b"qRcmd," + text.encode().hex().encode()))
        out = self.gdb.reply()
        self.assertTrue(out.startswith("O"), out)
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.reply(), "OK")
        return bytes.fromhex(out[1:]).decode()

    def test_help_status_mode_speed(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertIn("monitor reset", self.monitor("help"))
        self.assertIn("MSP430F5529, read-only mode, 250 kHz, halted at 0x04520, "
                      "hardware breakpoints 0 of 8 in use", self.monitor("status"))
        self.assertIn("not available in this version", self.monitor("mode full"))
        self.assertIn("read-only mode", self.monitor("mode readonly"))
        self.assertIn("500 kHz from the next attach", self.monitor("speed 500"))
        self.assertIn("100-1000", self.monitor("speed 5000"))
        self.assertIn("unknown monitor command", self.monitor("frobnicate"))

    def test_reset(self):
        self.start()
        self.assertIn("halted at 0x0441A", self.monitor("reset"))
        self.assertEqual([a[0] for a in self.fw.attaches], [0, 1])
        self.assertEqual(self.gdb.cmd("?"), "T0500:1a440000;")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```
